## assistant · 2026-08-08T15:42:06Z

Stopping again — I've now tried well over a dozen variants and this calibration is proving much harder than expected. Being straight with you rather than keep burning cycles.

**What's happening**: every fix I try breaks something else. Big one-shot shock → absorbed within one decision cycle. Sustained shock → moves price 5%, but nobody's holding enough position to notice. Forcing max-conviction signal on 20 agents → their positions stay tiny because `max_order_qty` throttles accumulation to ~50 lots/cycle. Raising their `max_order_qty` so they can build real size fast → they now provide so much of their own liquidity that my shock can't move price at all anymore. Delaying the shock to let them build first → same problem, their own resting orders re-absorb it.

**The doubtful assumption I should name**: I've been treating this as "find the right shock parameters" — but the real issue is that *position size* and *price impact* are fighting each other through the same liquidity pool, and tuning one knob at a time keeps trading one problem for the other. Getting this right needs either a more careful joint calculation (work out from the account math what price move + position size combination actually breaches 5% margin, then engineer exactly that, instead of trial-and-error), or accepting a much bigger time investment.

## assistant · 2026-08-08T15:45:48Z

Stopping here for real — this has gone from "calibration" to "the model has a genuine buy-the-dip feedback loop that fights back." Reporting exactly what happened rather than trying variant #20.

**What broke this attempt**: I gave forced agents a high `aggressiveness_bp` so their big buy orders would actually take liquidity instead of resting (which was absorbing my shock). That worked *too* well — with signal pinned at max conviction, each decision cycle's target position scales with current equity, and buying pushes price up, which raises their equity, which raises next cycle's target, which makes them buy more. It's a self-reinforcing loop that overpowered my sell-side shock entirely: positions reached **145,950 lots against a 100,000-lot wallet** (way past what should be admissible at 10x) and price went *up* to 10716, the opposite of what I needed.

My original linear account-math (position size × price move → margin ratio) was right, but it silently assumed position-building and price-shocking are independent. They're not — forcing one triggers feedback in the other. Untangling that properly needs deliberate modeling (e.g., cap the forced agents' equity growth, or freeze their target after one shot, or run the shock in a completely separate phase with no forced agents holding open positions yet), not more parameter guesses.

**Where things stand**: `bench/shock.py`'s `build_shock_series` (the "series of forcing trades instead of one" mechanism) is real, tested-by-hand infrastructure that genuinely moves price ~5% on its own — that part works and I'd keep it. ## assistant · 2026-08-08T15:51:53Z

E5/E6 标定探索到此为止，已提交并写进证据索引。总结一下今天这轮：

**成功落地的**：`ExperimentConfig.extra_events`/`extra_accounts` 通用挂钩（纯增量，不影响现有配置）+ `bench/shock.py` 的持续冲击机制（验证过能把价格推动约5%，比一次性大单有效）。

**没解决但搞清楚了原因**：建仓速度和价格冲击互相耦合——想让代理快速建大仓位就得放宽 `max_order_qty`/`aggressiveness_bp`，但这样它们自己的买盘会盖过冲击方向，还会触发"买盘推价格→推权益→推目标仓位→更多买盘"的真实正反馈环，仓位直接失控膨胀到 145,950 手（对 100,000 手钱包）。这需要把"建仓"和"冲击"分成互不干扰的两个阶段来设计，不是继续调参能解决的。

**当前状态**：E5/E6 仍诚实保持未勾选，过程写进了 `docs/experiments/0.1.2-exit-evidence-index.json`，作为下次继续标定的起点。全量测试 1113 passed，lint 干净，都已提交。

其余全部工作（CI 阈值、§6.2 全撤重报、T701-T705、T704、E1/E3/E4）已经真实完成，见前面的总结。今天先到这里。

## assistant · 2026-08-08T16:32:44Z

**E5 覆盖断言标定成功了。** 已推送到远程。

关键突破：不再让代理"决策循环建仓再冲击"（会失控反馈），改成直接把账户预置成已开仓的杠杆状态（复用案例7验证过的账户数学），配合持续冲击、错开各账户的强平阈值。真实 190 代理/100k 事务运行现在五项覆盖断言全部通过：强平 42、连锁强平 1、部分成交 76、撤单 27402、单边簿 76，结果可重复确定。

过程中还顺手挖出并修复了一个真 bug：`chain_depth` 因为 `_run_post_batch_risk_check` 从没把父链信息传下去，之前任何真实运行里都不可能出现连锁强平，即使底层逻辑早就实现且有单测——和 §6.2 是同一种"写了但没接线"的模式。

E5 现在是 `partially_met`：覆盖断言这半真达标了，性能标定（需要锁 CPU 到 P 核）那半还卡着，跟你之前说的一样需要你提供 P 核信息或你自己跑。测试 1127 passed，lint 干净。

E6（KPI-005/007/010/011 在真实研究运行上达标）还没动，是另一个范畴的事，E5 达标不会自动带来它达标。要继续往这个方向走，还是先做别的？

## user · 2026-08-08T16:37:48Z

性能标定为什么和交易有关系呢

## assistant · 2026-08-08T16:38:18Z

性能标定和"交易逻辑"本身没关系——它测的只是**跑仿真代码要多久**，锁核是为了让这个"多久"这个数字可信、能跨次比较。

具体原因：这台机器的 CPU（Meteor Lake）是混合架构，P 核（性能核）和 E 核（能效核）单线程速度能差将近一倍。同一份代码、同一份配置，如果这次系统把计时进程调度到 P 核、下次调度到 E 核，测出来的耗时能差 2 倍——这个差异跟你有没有改代码毫无关系，纯粹是"这次运气好分到了哪个核"。

如果不锁核，"归一化耗时 ≤ 10 秒"这个判断标准就没意义了：同一份代码可能这次测 6 秒（分到 P 核）、下次测 12 秒（分到 E 核），你没法用这个数字去判断"代码是不是变慢了"。锁核是为了排除这个噪音，让耗时的变化只反映代码本身的变化。

这跟我们刚修的 §6.2/chain_depth 那些交易逻辑 bug完全是两码事——那些是"结果对不对"，这个是"测速的这把尺子准不准"。

## user · 2026-08-08T16:41:20Z

那这个如何借助我的帮忙让你来做呢

## assistant · 2026-08-08T16:42:04Z

需要你做 3 件事，大概 5 分钟：

**1. 接上电源**（不是电池供电，`reference-machine.md` 要求外部电源）

**2. 切电源计划为"高性能"**
`控制面板 → 电源选项 → 高性能`（或 Windows 设置里的电源模式选"最佳性能"）

**3. 查一下哪几个逻辑处理器是 P 核**，最简单的方法：

```
Ctrl+Shift+Esc 打开任务管理器 → "性能"标签 → 点左边 "CPU"
→ 在图表上右键 → "更改图形为" → "逻辑处理器"
→ 鼠标悬停在每个小图上，看提示是否显示 "P 核"/"E 核"（P-core/E-core）
```

如果任务管理器不显示核心类型（旧版本可能不支持），告诉我一声，我给你一个更可靠的替代方法（比如推荐装个免费小工具 HWiNFO64 直接看拓扑）。

**你需要回给我的**：哪几个编号（0、1、2...）是 P 核。你这颗 U9 185H 是 6 个 P 核（应该占 12 个逻辑处理器，因为有超线程）+ 8 个 E 核 + 2 个低功耗 E 核，常见排列是 P 核在前（0-11），但不保证，所以要你确认一下实际显示的。

拿到核编号后，我就能用 PowerShell 把计时进程锁定到某个 P 核上，跑 5 次 CALIB-001 取中位数，正式写进 `reference-machine.md`。

## assistant · 2026-08-08T16:56:51Z

正式标定跑完了，但结果不对劲——需要你看一下。

**CALIB-001**（校准微基准）：中位数 0.29 秒，跟之前不锁核时（0.23 秒）差不多，正常。

**BENCH-001**（真实基准）：锁核后中位数 **24.9 秒**，比之前不锁核跑的 **6.5 秒**慢了将近 4 倍！而且 5 次结果本身波动很大（20.9~25.2 秒）。

这不太对——正常来说锁到 P 核应该跟不锁核差不多快甚至更快，不应该慢 4 倍。两种可能：

1. **锁错核了**：CPU-Z 里"物理核心 0-5"可能没有映射到 Windows 的逻辑处理器 0-11——比如这台机器的 BIOS/固件用了不同的编号方案，我实际上把进程锁到了 E 核甚至低功耗 E 核上。
2. **系统当前负载重，抢核**：把进程限制在 12 个线程里，如果你现在还开着浏览器/IDE 等其他吃 CPU 的东西，它们平时会被 Windows 自动调度去 E 核，现在可能被挤到跟我们抢同样这 12 个 P 核线程，造成争抢式变慢。

在没搞清楚是哪个原因之前，我不会把这个 24.9 秒写进 `reference-machine.md`——写错的参考值比没有更误导人（而且它本身已经超过了 10 秒门槛，如果是锁核锁错了导致的假象，会把"没问题的基准"误判成"性能不达标"）。

## assistant · 2026-08-08T18:25:55Z

**CI 全绿了。** 根因：CI 用 `pip install -e ".[dev]"` 装的是没锁版本的 `ruff>=0.6`，每次都拉 PyPI 最新——8 月 9 日那天最新是 0.16.2（我本地一直是 0.15.14），0.15→0.16 某个默认行为变了：markdown 里的代码块格式化以前要 `--preview` 才生效，现在默认开了。`docs/reviews/`、`conversations/` 下大量复盘报告/对话存档里贴的代码片段全被判定成"需要重新排版"——纯粹是 linter 版本漂移，不是这次改动引入的问题，本地一直是绿的正因为我固定用着旧版本没发现。

修了两处：
1. `pyproject.toml` 锁 `ruff>=0.16,<0.17`，以后 minor 版本升级带来的默认行为变化不会再无声把 CI 搞红。
2. 新增 `extend-exclude = ["*.md"]`——那些 markdown 里的代码片段是给人看的历史记录，不是维护中的源码，格式化它们没有意义还会破坏存档的原样性。`ruff check`（真正的 lint 检查）不受影响，只是 `format` 不再碰 markdown。

用 CI 同款的 0.16.2 在本地复现确认后推送，`gh run watch` 确认全部 4 个 job（真源校验、ruff、pytest×2 个 Python 版本）都通过了。

     ```python
     def build_zero_sum_declaration(
         accounts: dict[str, Account],
         initial_baseline: dict[str, int],
         exchange_fee_units: int,
         exchange_risk_pnl_units: int,
     ) -> ZeroSumDeclaration:
         per_agent_pnl = {
             aid: (a.wallet_units - a.entry_notional_units) - initial_baseline.get(aid, 0)
             for aid, a in accounts.items()
         }
         total_pnl = sum(per_agent_pnl.values())
         expected = -(exchange_fee_units + exchange_risk_pnl_units)
         residual = total_pnl - expected
         ...
     ```

     ```markdown
     ---
     name: ci-verify
     description: Run local lint/test checks before committing, then confirm CI is fully green after pushing — never report a task done with CI unchecked or red
     ---

     ## CI Verify

     A task involving a git commit is not "done" when `git push` succeeds. It is
     done when CI has actually finished and every job is green. Local checks
     passing does not guarantee CI passes — different environment, different
     dependency resolution, different tool versions.

     ### Before committing

     1. Find the project's local check commands. Look in order: a project
        `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md`, `package.json` scripts, `Makefile`, or the CI
        workflow file itself (`.github/workflows/*.yml`, etc.) — the CI config is
        the ground truth for what "passing" means.
     2. Run those commands locally (lint, format check, type check, test suite —
        whatever the project defines). Fix everything before committing; don't
        push speculatively and let CI find the problems.
     3. If a check tool is only loosely version-pinned in the project (e.g.
        `ruff>=0.6` with no upper bound), be aware the CI-installed version can
        differ from the locally-installed one and silently disagree — see the
        diagnostic note below.

     ### After pushing

     1. Get the run for this push: `gh run list --limit 1` (or filter by branch/
        workflow if there could be ambiguity with concurrent pushes).
     2. Watch it to completion: `gh run watch <run-id> --exit-status`. Do not
        poll manually or guess — wait for the real result.
     3. Confirm **every** job in the run succeeded, not just the one you expect
        to be affected by your change (e.g. a docs-only change can still break a
        job you didn't think to check).
     4. If any job is red, treat the task as still in progress: read the failure
        log (`gh run view <run-id> --log-failed`), fix the root cause, commit,
        push, and repeat step 1–3. Only report the task complete once a run
        triggered by the current HEAD is fully green.

     ### Diagnostic tip: "local green, CI red, and the failure is unrelated to my diff"

     This pattern usually means environment/dependency drift, not a bug in the
     change just made. Before searching further:

     - Compare the locally installed version of the failing tool against what
       the project's dependency file would resolve to fresh (e.g. `ruff --version`
       locally vs. the version range in `pyproject.toml`/`package.json`). CI
       typically does a clean install every run and will pick up new releases of
       loosely-pinned dev dependencies that a local environment — often installed
       once, months ago — never picked up.
     - If that's the cause, the durable fix is to pin the dependency to a bounded
       range (not just a floor) in the project's manifest, verify locally with
       that exact version, and only then commit the pin change alongside any
       config adjustments the new version requires.
     ```
